existing_test_function_signature: |
  @Test
  public void testPrintWithEscapingEnabledNoEscapeChar() throws IOException {
    // existing test code
  }
new_tests:
- test_behavior: |
    Test that the CSVPrinter outputs the correct format when escaping is enabled but no escape character is set.
  test_name: |
    testPrintWithEscapingEnabledNoEscapeChar
  test_code: |
    @Test
    public void testPrintWithEscapingEnabledNoEscapeChar() throws IOException {
        StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
        CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(null));
        printer.printRecord("value1", "value2");
        printer.println(); // Ensure to add a new line after the record

language: java
existing_test_function_signature: public void testWithHeaderNull()
new_tests:
  - test_behavior: Test handling of null header values.
    test_name: testWithHeaderNull
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testWithHeaderNull() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withHeader((String[]) null);
          assertNull(format.getHeader());
      }
  - test_behavior: Test equality comparison with different quote modes.
    test_name: testEqualsWithDifferentQuoteModes
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testEqualsWithDifferentQuoteModes() {
          CSVFormat format1 = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.ALL);
          CSVFormat format2 = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withQuoteMode(QuoteMode.NONE);
          assertFalse(format1.equals(format2));
      }
  - test_behavior: Test behavior with a comment marker set.
    test_name: testIsCommentMarkerSet
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testIsCommentMarkerSet() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withCommentMarker('#');
          assertTrue(format.isCommentMarkerSet());
      }
  - test_behavior: Test escape character handling in output.
    test_name: testPrintAndEscape
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testPrintAndEscape() throws IOException {
          StringWriter writer = new StringWriter();
          CSVPrinter printer = new CSVPrinter(writer, CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withEscape(BACKSLASH));
          printer.printRecord("Value with newline\nand comma,");
          String output = writer.toString();
          assertTrue(output.contains("Value with newline\\n"));
          assertTrue(output.contains("and comma,"));
      }
  - test_behavior: Test null string handling during formatting.
    test_name: testFormatWithNullValue
    test_code: |
      @Test
      public void testFormatWithNullValue() {
          CSVFormat format = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.withNullString("NULL");
          String result = format.format("Value", null, "Another Value");
          assertTrue(result.contains("NULL"));
      }

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test handling of null header in CSVFormat.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
    entry_hint: |
      public CSVFormat withHeader(String... header)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withHeader() with a null header array.
      2. The constructor is invoked, leading to the header assignment.
      3. The branch handling null header is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Pass a null value to the withHeader() method.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the resulting CSVFormat instance has a null header without throwing an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test equality comparison with different quote modes.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public boolean equals(Object obj)
    entry_hint: |
      public CSVFormat withQuoteMode(QuoteMode quoteModePolicy)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Create two CSVFormat instances with different quote modes.
      2. Call equals() to compare the two instances.
      3. The branch checking for quote mode differences is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Create two CSVFormat objects, one with QuoteMode.ALL and the other with QuoteMode.NONE.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the equals() method returns false when comparing the two instances.

  - design_name: |
      Test escaping behavior with special characters.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void printAndEscape(CharSequence value, int offset, int len, Appendable out)
    entry_hint: |
      public void printRecord(Appendable out, Object... values)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call printRecord() with a value containing special characters (e.g., newline, comma).
      2. The printAndEscape() method is invoked during printing.
      3. The branch handling special character escaping is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a value that includes a newline character and a comma.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the output correctly escapes the special characters according to the CSV format rules.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when record separator is set to null.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(String recordSeparator)
    entry_hint: |
      public CSVFormat withSystemRecordSeparator()
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withSystemRecordSeparator() to set the record separator to the system default.
      2. The constructor is invoked, leading to the record separator assignment.
      3. The branch handling null record separator is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Ensure that the system's line separator is used as the record separator.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the resulting CSVFormat instance uses the system's line separator correctly.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when null string is set for null values.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat withNullString(String nullString)
    entry_hint: |
      public CSVFormat withNullString(String nullString)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withNullString() with a specific string to represent null values.
      2. The constructor is invoked, leading to the null string assignment.
      3. The branch handling null string assignment is executed.
    input_focus: |
      Pass a specific string (e.g., "N/A") to represent null values.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the CSVFormat instance correctly uses the specified string for null values during parsing and formatting.

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when header is null during CSVFormat construction.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public static CSVFormat newFormat(final char delimiter)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call newFormat() with a valid delimiter.
      2. The constructor is reached, where header is set to null.
    input_focus: |
      Use a valid delimiter character, such as ','.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the resulting CSVFormat instance has a null header and does not throw an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test equality check with different quote modes.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        @Override public boolean equals(final Object obj)
      entry_hint: |
        public static CSVFormat valueOf(final String format)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call valueOf() to get a CSVFormat instance.
      2. Create another CSVFormat instance with a different quote mode.
      3. Call equals() to compare the two instances.
    input_focus: |
      Create two CSVFormat instances with the same parameters except for the quote mode.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the equality check returns false due to differing quote modes.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when escape character is set to null.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withEscape(final Character escape)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withEscape() with null as the escape character.
      2. The constructor is reached, where escapeCharacter is set to null.
    input_focus: |
      Start from a CSVFormat instance with a defined delimiter and quote character.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the resulting CSVFormat instance has a null escape character and does not throw an exception.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when null string is set to a valid value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withNullString(final String nullString)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withNullString() with a valid non-null string.
      2. The constructor is reached, where nullString is set to the provided value.
    input_focus: |
      Use a valid delimiter and quote character while setting a non-null string for null representation.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the resulting CSVFormat instance reflects the provided null string correctly.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when record separator is set to a custom value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(final String recordSeparator)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withRecordSeparator() with a custom string as the record separator.
      2. The constructor is reached, where recordSeparator is set to the provided value.
    input_focus: |
      Start from a CSVFormat instance with a defined delimiter and quote character.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the resulting CSVFormat instance reflects the custom record separator correctly.

```yaml
test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when header is null during CSVFormat initialization.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public static CSVFormat newFormat(final char delimiter)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call newFormat() with a valid delimiter.
      2. The constructor is invoked with a null header.
      3. The internal state is set, and the object is created.
    input_focus: |
      Use a valid delimiter character (e.g., ',') and pass null for the header.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the CSVFormat instance is created without throwing exceptions and that the header is indeed set to null.

  - design_name: |
      Test equality comparison with different quote modes.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public boolean equals(final Object obj)
      entry_hint: |
        public static CSVFormat valueOf(final String format)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call valueOf() to get a CSVFormat instance.
      2. Create another CSVFormat instance with a different quote mode.
      3. Call equals() to compare the two instances.
    input_focus: |
      Create two CSVFormat instances with the same properties except for the quote mode.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the equals() method returns false when comparing the two instances.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when escape character is set to the same as the delimiter.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withEscape(final Character escape)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withEscape() with a character that is the same as the delimiter.
      2. The validate() method is invoked during the construction of the new CSVFormat.
    input_focus: |
      Use a delimiter character (e.g., ',') and set the escape character to the same character.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown due to the invalid configuration.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when the record separator is set to an invalid value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withRecordSeparator(final String recordSeparator)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withRecordSeparator() with an invalid separator string.
      2. The validate() method is invoked during the construction of the new CSVFormat.
    input_focus: |
      Provide a string that does not match the expected line break characters (e.g., "invalid").
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that an IllegalArgumentException is thrown indicating the invalid record separator.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when null string is set to a non-null value.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private void validate()
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withNullString(final String nullString)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withNullString() with a valid non-null string.
      2. The validate() method is invoked during the construction of the new CSVFormat.
    input_focus: |
      Use a non-null string (e.g., "NULL") for the null string configuration.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the CSVFormat instance is created successfully and that the null string is set correctly.
```

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when header is null during CSVFormat creation.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public static CSVFormat newFormat(final char delimiter)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call newFormat() with a valid delimiter.
      2. The constructor is invoked, where header is set to null.
      3. Validate method checks the header for null.
    input_focus: |
      Use a valid delimiter character, e.g., ','.
    observable_behavior: |
      Ensure that the CSVFormat instance is created without throwing exceptions and that the header remains null.

  - design_name: |
      Test equality check with different quote modes.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        public boolean equals(final Object obj)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withQuoteMode(QuoteMode quoteModePolicy)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Create two CSVFormat instances with different quote modes.
      2. Call equals() on one instance with the other as an argument.
      3. The comparison logic checks the quote mode.
    input_focus: |
      Instantiate two CSVFormat objects, one with QuoteMode.ALL and the other with QuoteMode.NONE.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the equality check returns false due to differing quote modes.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when escape character is set to null.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withEscape(final Character escape)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withEscape() with null as the escape character.
      2. The constructor is invoked, setting escapeCharacter to null.
      3. Validate method checks for escape character presence.
    input_focus: |
      Use a valid delimiter and set the escape character to null.
    observable_behavior: |
      Ensure that the CSVFormat instance is created without throwing exceptions and that the escape character is indeed null.

  - design_name: |
      Test handling of a null comment marker.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private CSVFormat(char delimiter, Character quoteChar, QuoteMode quoteMode, Character commentStart, Character escape, boolean ignoreSurroundingSpaces, boolean ignoreEmptyLines, String recordSeparator, String nullString, Object[] headerComments, String[] header, boolean skipHeaderRecord, boolean allowMissingColumnNames, boolean ignoreHeaderCase, boolean trim, boolean trailingDelimiter, boolean autoFlush)
      entry_hint: |
        public CSVFormat withCommentMarker(final Character commentMarker)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call withCommentMarker() with null as the comment marker.
      2. The constructor is invoked, setting commentMarker to null.
      3. Validate method checks for comment marker presence.
    input_focus: |
      Use a valid delimiter and set the comment marker to null.
    observable_behavior: |
      Ensure that the CSVFormat instance is created without throwing exceptions and that the comment marker is indeed null.

      Test behavior when record separator is set to an unsupported value.

test_designs:
  - design_name: |
      Test equality comparison with different quote characters.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean equals(Object obj)
      entry_hint: |
        public boolean isEqual(CSVFormat other)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call isEqual() with a CSVFormat instance that has a different quote character.
      2. The equals() method is invoked, reaching the branch that checks quoteCharacter.
      3. The comparison evaluates to false due to differing quote characters.
    input_focus: |
      Create a CSVFormat instance with a specific quote character and compare it to another instance with a different quote character.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the equality check returns false when the quote characters differ.

  - design_name: |
      Test equality comparison with null comment marker.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean equals(Object obj)
      entry_hint: |
        public boolean isEqual(CSVFormat other)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call isEqual() with a CSVFormat instance that has a null comment marker.
      2. The equals() method is invoked, reaching the branch that checks commentMarker.
      3. The comparison evaluates to true if both have null comment markers or false if one is null.
    input_focus: |
      Create two CSVFormat instances, one with a null comment marker and the other with a non-null comment marker, and compare them.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the equality check behaves correctly based on the presence or absence of the comment marker.

  - design_name: |
      Test hash code generation with different escape characters.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private int hashCode()
      entry_hint: |
        public int getHashCode()
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call getHashCode() on a CSVFormat instance with a specific escape character.
      2. The hashCode() method is invoked, reaching the branch that includes escapeCharacter in the hash calculation.
      3. The hash code is computed based on the escape character value.
    input_focus: |
      Create two CSVFormat instances with different escape characters and compute their hash codes.
    observable_behavior: |
      Verify that the hash codes differ when the escape characters are different.

  - design_name: |
      Test behavior when ignoring empty lines is toggled.
    method_context:
      method_signature_hint: |
        private boolean equals(Object obj)
      entry_hint: |
        public boolean isEqual(CSVFormat other)
    execution_flow: |
      1. Call isEqual() with a CSVFormat instance that has ignoreEmptyLines set to true.
      2. The equals() method is invoked, reaching the branch that checks ignoreEmptyLines.
      3. The comparison evaluates to true if both instances have the same ignoreEmptyLines setting.
    input_focus: |
      Create two CSVFormat instances, one with ignoreEmptyLines set to true and the other set to false, and compare them.
    observable_behavior: |
      Check that the equality check reflects the ignoreEmptyLines setting correctly.

    input_focus: |
